     Aşama 1: Açık konular

     1.1 Doğrulama isteği (internal/retry, internal/queue)

     - Mevcut confirms mantığı kalır. Doğrulama isteği normal bir claim'dir,
       yani dispatcher'daki lim.Wait'ten (rate limiter) geçer. Bunu koddaki
       yorumda ve DECISIONS'ta açıkça yazarım.
     - Decide: attempt == MaxTotalAttempts() ve sonuç yanıtsız (Transient + Err)
       ise Fail döner. last_error:
       outcome unknown: attempt 5 and the confirmation request with the same 
       idempotency key both ended without an answer (timeout); the provider may 
       have delivered the message.
       Doğrulamaya 5xx/429 gelirse bu bir yanıttır: normal "gave up after 6
       attempts: http 500".
     - RecoverLeases: doğrulama denemesinde lease dolarsa outcome unknown: lease
       expired on the confirmation request ... yazılır.
     - Testler:
       - retry_test tablo satırları: attempt 6 timeout, attempt 6 connection
         error, attempt 6 için 500.
       - Integration: provider bağlantıyı hep koparır, attempts=4'ten başlanır →
         teslimat failed olur, last_error outcome unknown ile başlar, provider
         aynı key ile tam 2 istek görür.
     - DECISIONS başlığı (İngilizce kuralı gereği):
       "At most 5 send attempts; if the outcome is unknown, 1 confirmation
       request with the same idempotency key". Open issue'daki maddeler buraya
       taşınır.

     1.2 UUID idempotency key

     - Migration 0003_delivery_keys_and_breaker.sql:
       ALTER TABLE deliveries ADD COLUMN idempotency_key UUID NOT NULL DEFAULT 
       gen_random_uuid() + UNIQUE. Volatile default mevcut satırları tek tek
       doldurur.
     - Claim sorgusu d.idempotency_key::text döner. Delivery.Key() bu alanı
       kullanır. Yeni bağımlılık yok.
     - Güncellenecek yerler:
       - worker_integration_test.go içindeki ids() /
         requireProviderMatchesDatabase artık idempotency_key seçer.
       - soak key/id karşılaştırması da aynı şekilde güncellenir.
     - Test: DB sıfırlanıp id'ler 1'den başladığında aynı provider örneğine
       giden yeni teslimatlar duplicate sayılmaz (iki testdb, tek provider).

     1.3 Kanal başına devre kesici (internal/worker/breaker.go)

     - Saf durum makinesi, saat enjekte edilir: closed → open → half-open.
       - Art arda Threshold sayıda (varsayılan 10) yanıtsız hata ya da 5xx
         devreyi açar.
       - Cooldown (varsayılan 10 s) dolunca tek bir deneme isteğine izin
         verilir. Başarılıysa devre kapanır, değilse yeniden açılır.
       - Teslim edilen istek ve 4xx (provider ayakta demektir) sayacı sıfırlar.
         429 nötrdür.
     - Dispatcher token'dan ve claim'den önce breaker.Allow() sorar. Devre
       açıkken claim yapılmaz.
     - İade: devre kapalı değilken dönen bağlantı hatası ya da 5xx (uçuştaki
       istekler ve deneme isteği) Finish ile queued'a döner, attempts = attempts
       - 1 olur. Fence yine WHERE attempts = $mine ile yapılır.
       Bu yüzden kesinti sürdükçe hiçbir teslimat failed olmaz. Kampanya bu
       sürede 'sending'de kalır; bu durum metrikte görünür.
     - Backoff: DefaultPolicy BaseDelay 15 s, MaxDelay 2 dk. Dört bekleme equal
       jitter ile toplam yaklaşık 112–225 s, yani birkaç dakikalık bir retry
       penceresi. retry_test sayıları güncellenir.
       Soak'un bekleme süresi uzarsa soak timeout'unu kontrol eder, gerekirse
       artırırım.
     - Devre durumu ve heartbeat tablosu (migration 0003'e eklenir):
       worker_channel_status (channel TEXT PRIMARY KEY CHECK (channel IN 
       ('sms','email')), circuit TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (circuit IN 
       ('closed','open','half_open')), last_error TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '', 
       updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()).
       - Kanalın dispatcher'ı (yani o kanalın advisory lock'unu tutan worker)
         her HeartbeatInterval (varsayılan 2 s) ve her devre değişiminde INSERT 
         ... ON CONFLICT (channel) DO UPDATE ile satırı yazar. updated_at DB
         saatinden gelir (now()), worker saati kullanılmaz.
       - last_error kısa ve sır içermeyen metindir (retry paketindeki describe
         çıktısı, 500 karakterle sınırlı). Devre kapanınca boşaltılır.
       - Kilidi alamayan yedek worker o kanal için yazmaz; böylece iki worker
         birbirinin durumunu ezmez.
       - Yazma başarısız olursa loglanır ve sayılır
         (scc_worker_heartbeat_errors_total); gönderimi durdurmaz. Heartbeat
         yazılamıyorsa satır eskir ve UI doğru biçimde "worker çalışmıyor"
         gösterir.
       - Worker metriği (scc_worker_circuit_state) da kalır.
       - internal/queue içinde SetChannelStatus ve ChannelStatuses fonksiyonları
         (saf SQL, ChannelStatuses yaşı DB'de EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM now() - 
         updated_at) ile hesaplar).
     - Aynı open issue'dan küçük bir ek: worker açılışta PROVIDER_URL için şema
       ve host doğrular.
     - Testler:
       - Breaker için sahte saatle tablo testi.
       - Integration: provider'ın önünde bir "kapalı" sarmalayıcı durur. 10 s
         bağlantıyı hijack edip kapatır, 10 s 503 döner, sonra açılır.
         fastRetry ile çalışır (kesici olmasa her şey failed olurdu), Cooldown
         300 ms enjekte edilir.
         Beklenen: hiçbir teslimat failed değil, kampanya completed, provider =
         DB key key eşleşmesi. Süre yaklaşık 25 s.
         Aynı testte kesinti sırasında worker_channel_status satırı open ve boş
         olmayan last_error gösterir; açılınca closed ve boş last_error olur.
       - Heartbeat integration testi: HeartbeatInterval 100 ms enjekte edilir;
         satır güncellenir (updated_at ilerler); worker durdurulunca ilerlemez;
         yedek worker kilitli kanala yazmaz.

     1.4 Her şeyi eşleyen segment

     - segment.Rule.Channels alanı eklenir. Şemada channels: sms/email enum
       dizisi, zorunlu. Boş dizi "brief kanal belirtmiyor" demektir, yani iki
       kanal da açık.
     - Bağlantılı güncellemeler:
       - Decode/Validate (tekrar ve bilinmeyen değer kontrolü)
       - şema drift testleri
       - segment test fixture'ları ve testdb.AllUsersRule
     - Prompt (compose/prompts.go):
       - "İfade edemediğin kriter için uydurma koşul üretme,
         unsupported_criteria'ya yaz" talimatı açıkça eklenir.
       - Kanal talimatı eklenir.
       - "Hiçbir şey ifade edilemezse language in [tr,en]" yedeği kalır, çünkü
         kural boş olamaz. Artık bu durum işaretlenir.
       - prompts_test bu talimatları arar.
     - Demo mock: "sms" / "e-posta|email|mail" anahtar kelimeleri channels'ı
       doldurur. "Kriter yok" senaryosu her şeyi eşleyen kural + unsupported
       döner; test edilir.
     - Saf campaign.Warnings(audience, unsupported):
       - matches_everyone (matched == total_users > 0)
       - unsupported_criteria (details: liste)
       - Tablo testi bu aşamada yazılır. API alanı ve onay teyidi 2. aşamada
         bağlanır.

     1.5 LLM_MODE (internal/llm)

     - FromEnv(getenv) (Client, error) şöyle davranır:
       - auto (varsayılan): key varsa anthropic, yoksa mock.
       - anthropic: key yoksa hata.
       - mock: key olsa da mock.
       - Başka bir değer: hata.
     - api açılışta mod, model ve seçim nedenini loglar. Mod /api/config'te
       döner.
     - llm_test: tüm kombinasyonlar için tablo testi.

     1.6 Kanal başına advisory lock

     - queue.AcquireChannel(ctx, channel): havuzdan ayrılmış bir bağlantı
       üzerinde pg_try_advisory_lock(ns int4, channel int4) çalıştırır. İki
       int'li anahtar, migrate/seed'in bigint anahtarlarıyla çakışmaz.
     - Dispatcher'ın claim'leri bu bağlantı üzerinden yapılır. Bağlantı koparsa
       kilit de gider ve claim hata verir. Dispatcher kilidi yeniden almaya
       döner, iki worker'ın aynı kanalda gönderim yapması önlenir.
     - Kilidi alamayan worker LockRetryInterval aralığıyla yeniden dener
       (varsayılan 5 s, testte 200 ms). Maintenance döngüsü iki worker'da da
       çalışabilir, idempotenttir.
     - Integration testi (tek):
       - İki worker çalışır, busiest second ≤ 100/kanal.
       - Kanalı yalnızca biri tutar.
       - Birincisi durunca ikincisi devralır ve kampanyayı bitirir.

     ---

     Aşama 2: Kampanya API'si

     Yeni bağımlılıklar

     github.com/go-chi/chi/v5 ve github.com/prometheus/client_golang.
     Container'da go list -m -versions ile güncel kararlı sürümü bulur,
     go.mod/go.sum'da tam sürümle sabitlerim. Nedeni DECISIONS'a yazılır. UUID
     için kütüphane gerekmiyor, değeri DB üretiyor.

     Migration 0004_campaign_api.sql

     - campaigns'e eklenecek kolonlar:
       - version INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1
       - audience_matched, audience_sms, audience_email, audience_total_users
         (INT), audience_counted_at
       - snapshot: approved_rule JSONB, approved_content JSONB,
         approved_channels TEXT[]
       - CHECK: approved ve sonrası durumlarda snapshot dolu
     - Worker Claim ve Enqueue artık approved_* kolonlarını okur. Böylece donmuş
       içerik canlı kolondan ayrı tutulur.
       testdb.AddApprovedCampaign ve soak'un kampanya insert'i buna göre
       güncellenir.
     - Stats ve tamamlanma kontrolü için index: deliveries (campaign_id, status,
       channel, attempts).
       - Kolon sırası bilerek status ikinci: CompleteFinished (campaign_id = 
         c.id AND status IN ('queued','sending') ve status = 'failed' aramaları)
         (campaign_id, status) prefix'ini kullanır; stats sorgusu (WHERE 
         campaign_id = $1 GROUP BY channel, status, attempts > 0) dört kolonun
         hepsini içerdiği için index-only scan yapabilir.
       - Eski deliveries_campaign_status_idx (campaign_id, status) yeni index'in
         prefix'i olduğu için aynı migration'da drop edilir (yeni index önce
         yaratılır, sonra eskisi düşer).
       - EXPLAIN doğrulaması: bir integration testi (queue_integration_test)
         20.000 satır ekler, VACUUM ANALYZE deliveries çalıştırır, sonra iki
         sorgu için EXPLAIN (FORMAT JSON) alır ve plan JSON'unda yeni index'in
         adının geçtiğini (Index Only Scan / Index Scan, Seq Scan değil) kontrol
         eder: (a) stats sorgusu, (b) tamamlanma kontrolündeki NOT EXISTS ... 
         status IN ('queued','sending') alt sorgusu. Sonuçlar implementasyon
         sırasında elle de EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS) ile çalıştırılıp çıktı
         raporda gösterilir. Planner küçük tabloda seq scan seçebileceği için
         testte satır sayısı yüksek tutulur ve gerekirse SET LOCAL 
         enable_seqscan = off ile yalnızca index'in kullanılabilirliği
         kanıtlanır; bu durumda DECISIONS'a yazılır.

     internal/campaign

     - state.go: Next(from State, a Action) (State, error) saf fonksiyon. Hata
       ErrInvalidTransition.

     ┌─────────────────────┬────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────┐
     │       Action        │  İzinli durumlar   │           Sonraki            │
     ├─────────────────────┼────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────┤
     │                     │ draft,             │ draft→audience_ready,        │
     │ GenerateSegment     │ audience_ready,    │ diğerleri aynı kalır (içerik │
     │                     │ content_ready      │  korunur)                    │
     ├─────────────────────┼────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────┤
     │ SetChannels         │ audience_ready,    │ aynı                         │
     │                     │ content_ready      │                              │
     ├─────────────────────┼────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────┤
     │ SaveContent (stream │ audience_ready,    │ content_ready                │
     │  sonucu ya da PUT)  │ content_ready      │                              │
     ├─────────────────────┼────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────┤
     │ Approve             │ content_ready      │ approved (aynı tx'te Enqueue │
     │                     │                    │  → sending / completed)      │
     └─────────────────────┴────────────────────┴──────────────────────────────┘

     - store.go (pgx):
       - Her değişiklik şu sırayla yapılır: yükle → Next → UPDATE ... SET 
         status=$next, version=version+1 WHERE id=$1 AND status=$loaded AND 
         version=$loaded_v RETURNING ....
       - 0 satır güncellenirse 409 state_changed döner.
       - Stream'in sonucu kaydedilirken satır yeniden yüklenir. Stream sırasında
         onay verildiyse kayıt reddedilir.
     - Approve(ctx, id, version, ack):
       - tek tx, SELECT ... FOR UPDATE
       - Next ile durum kontrolü
       - version uyuşmazsa → ErrVersionMismatch
       - uyarı varsa ve ack yoksa → ErrAcknowledgementRequired
       - segment ve içerik bir kez daha doğrulanır
       - snapshot kolonları yazılır, approved_at = now()
       - queue.EnqueueTx(ctx, tx, id) çalışır (mevcut Enqueue bunun ince bir
         sarmalayıcısı olarak kalır)
     - audience.go: tek sorgu. count(*), count(*) FILTER (WHERE u.sms_consent),
       ... email_consent ve (SELECT count(*) FROM users), segment.Compile(rule, 
       now, 1) ile.
     - stats.go: SELECT channel, status, attempts > 0, count(*) ... WHERE 
       campaign_id=$1 GROUP BY 1,2,3 (index'li).

     internal/api (chi)

     - Middleware:
       - RequestID
       - slog istek logu
       - JSON hata zarfıyla panic recover
       - Prometheus süre ölçümü (route pattern ile)
       - http.MaxBytesReader 64 KiB
     - JSON decode DisallowUnknownFields ile yapılır. CORS yok.
     - Hata gövdesi her yerde {"error":{"code","message","details"}}. 404/405 da
       bu biçimde döner.
     - Brief: boş olmamalı, en fazla 2.000 karakter (rune).
     - LLM eşzamanlılık kapısı:
       - Görev başına semafor, LLM_MAX_CONCURRENCY (varsayılan 2).
       - Yer açılmasını en fazla 5 s bekler, sonra 503 llm_busy + Retry-After
         döner.
       - Görev zaman sınırları: segment 2 dk, içerik 5 dk. Bu, compose'daki
         "görev seviyesinde süre sınırı yok" open issue'sunu kapatır.
     - Metrikler, LLM tarafında bir llm.Client sarmalayıcısıyla toplanır: her
       çağrı için sonuç ve süre.
     - Kampanya başına üretim kilidi: bellek içi TryLock. Tek api instance
       varsayımı DECISIONS'a yazılır.
     - SSE:
       - Yeniden bağlanma koruması: tarayıcıdaki EventSource bağlantı kopunca
         kendiliğinden yeniden bağlanır (ve done'dan sonra sunucu akışı
         kapatınca da). Bu yüzden kampanya content_ready'deyse stream yalnızca
         ?regenerate=true ile başlar; yoksa 409 content_exists. Elle yapılmış
         düzenlemeler ve kredi, otomatik yeniden bağlanmayla harcanmaz. Ek
         önlemler: akışın ilk satırı retry: 3600000 (yeniden bağlanma gecikmesi
         1 saat); UI done/error event'inde EventSource'u kendisi kapatır
         (frontend notu).
       - Düzenleme yarışı: stream başlarken yüklenen version saklanır; sonuç
         yalnızca WHERE status = ... AND version = <yüklenen> ile kaydedilir.
         Stream sürerken araya giren bir PUT /content (ya da başka değişiklik)
         kazanır; stream error {code: state_changed} ile biter ve elle yapılan
         düzenleme ezilmez.
       - İlk üretim (audience_ready) sırasında bağlantı kopup yeniden bağlanırsa
         üretim yeniden başlar (kayıt hiç olmadığı için kayıp yoktur). Bu durum
         DECISIONS'a yazılır; kredi kaybı en fazla bir yarım üretimdir ve
         generation_in_progress kilidi eşzamanlı çifti engeller.
       - Üretim goroutine'i event'leri tamponlu bir kanala (256) bırakır. Tampon
         dolarsa üretim iptal edilir ve istemci yavaş sayılır. Böylece onEvent
         SDK okuma döngüsünü bloklamaz (open issue).
       - Handler event'leri yazıp flush eder, 15 s'de bir : ping gönderir.
       - r.Context() iptal olursa LLM isteği de iptal olur.

     API sözleşmesi

     Uç: POST /api/campaigns
     İstek: {"brief": string}
     Başarılı yanıt: 201 Campaign
     Hata durumları: 400 invalid_json · 413 body_too_large · 422
     validation_failed
       (brief) · LLM hataları (aşağıda; kampanya draft olarak kalır,
       details.campaign_id döner)
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     Uç: GET /api/campaigns
     İstek: ?limit=1..200 (varsayılan 50)
     Başarılı yanıt: 200 {"campaigns":[Summary]} (id desc)
     Hata durumları: 422 validation_failed
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     Uç: GET /api/campaigns/{id}
     İstek: –
     Başarılı yanıt: 200 Campaign
     Hata durumları: 404 not_found
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     Uç: POST /api/campaigns/{id}/segment
     İstek: gövde yok
     Başarılı yanıt: 200 Campaign (yeni kural ve sayılar, version+1)
     Hata durumları: 404 · 409 invalid_state · LLM hataları
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     Uç: PUT /api/campaigns/{id}/channels
     İstek: {"channels":["sms","email"]}
     Başarılı yanıt: 200 Campaign
     Hata durumları: 422 validation_failed (boş / bilinmeyen / tekrar) · 404 ·
     409
       invalid_state
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     Uç: GET /api/campaigns/{id}/content/stream
     İstek: SSE; ?regenerate=true (yalnızca content_ready'de gerekir)
     Başarılı yanıt: 200 text/event-stream: retry: 3600000 → attempt {attempt} →

       delta {field,text}* / raw {text} → done {campaign} ya da error 
       {error:{code,message,details}}
     Hata durumları: Akış başlamadan JSON döner: 404 · 409 invalid_state · 409 
       content_exists (content_ready'de regenerate=true yoksa; LLM çağrılmaz,
     içerik
        değişmez) · 409 generation_in_progress · 422 validation_failed
     (regenerate
       true/false dışında) · 503 llm_busy
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     Uç: PUT /api/campaigns/{id}/content
     İstek: {"sms","email_subject","email_body"}
     Başarılı yanıt: 200 Campaign (sms_info: encoding, units, segments)
     Hata durumları: 422 validation_failed, details: [{path, message}] · 404 ·
     409
       invalid_state
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     Uç: GET /api/campaigns/{id}/preview
     İstek: ?limit=1..10 (varsayılan 3)
     Başarılı yanıt: 200 {"samples":[{user:{id,first_name,city}, sms, 
     email_subject,
        email_body}]} (segmentin ilk n kullanıcısı, content.Render ile)
     Hata durumları: 404 · 409 invalid_state (içerik yok)
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     Uç: POST /api/campaigns/{id}/approve
     İstek: {"version": int, "acknowledge_warnings": bool}
     Başarılı yanıt: 200 {"campaign":Campaign, "enqueued":{"sms":n,"email":n}}
       (status sending ya da completed)
     Hata durumları: 409 invalid_state (ikinci onay dahil) · 409
     version_mismatch
       (details: current_version) · 422 acknowledgement_required (details:
     warnings)
        · 404
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     Uç: GET /api/campaigns/{id}/stats
     İstek: –
     Başarılı yanıt: 200
       {"campaign_id","status","channels":{"sms":{"queued","sending","retrying",
     "sent","failed","skipped","total","provider":{"state","circuit","last_error
     ","updated_at","age_seconds"}},"email":{...}}}
     Hata durumları: 404
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     Uç: GET /api/config
     İstek: –
     Başarılı yanıt: 200
       {"llm_mode","model","limits":{"sms":160,"email_subject":80,"email_body":2
     000,"brief":2000},"variables":["first_name","city"],"channels":["sms","emai
     l"]}
     Hata durumları: –
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     Uç: GET /healthz · /readyz · /metrics
     İstek: –
     Başarılı yanıt: 200 · 200 / 503 (DB ping) · Prometheus metin
     Hata durumları: –

     - Campaign:
       - id, brief, status, version, channels
       - segment {conditions, explanation, unsupported_criteria, 
         requested_channels}
       - audience {matched, sms, email, total_users, counted_at}
       - warnings [{code,message,details}]
       - content | null, sms_info | null
       - approved_at, completed_at, created_at, updated_at
     - LLM hata eşlemesi:

     ┌───────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────────────────┐
     │           Hata            │                    Yanıt                    │
     ├───────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────┤
     │ Refusal                   │ 422 llm_refused                             │
     ├───────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────┤
     │ RateLimitError            │ 429 llm_rate_limited + Retry-After başlığı  │
     │                           │ + details.retry_after_seconds               │
     ├───────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────┤
     │ Timeout ya da görev       │ 504 llm_timeout                             │
     │ süresinin dolması         │                                             │
     ├───────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────┤
     │ ErrUnavailable            │ 503 llm_unavailable                         │
     ├───────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────┤
     │ ErrRepairExhausted        │ 502 llm_invalid_output (details: problems)  │
     ├───────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────┤
     │ ErrMaxTokens              │ 502 llm_output_truncated                    │
     ├───────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────┤
     │ ErrBadRequest ya da       │ 502 llm_error (iç ayrıntı loglanır, yanıtta │
     │ ErrUnexpectedStop         │  yer almaz)                                 │
     ├───────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────┤
     │ Diğer                     │ 500 internal (request id ile)               │
     └───────────────────────────┴─────────────────────────────────────────────┘

     - Retrying ile queued ayrık sayılır: queued = attempts 0, retrying = queued
       ve attempts > 0.
     - provider.state (UI'nın göstereceği metni belirler; yalnızca kanalda henüz
       tamamlanmamış teslimat varken anlamlıdır, ama alan her zaman döner):

     ┌──────────────────────┬──────────────────────────┬────────────────────────
     ┐
     │        state         │          Koşul           │    UI metni (örnek)
     │
     ├──────────────────────┼──────────────────────────┼────────────────────────
     ┤
     │ ok                   │ satır yeni ve circuit =  │ –
     │
     │                      │ closed                   │
     │
     ├──────────────────────┼──────────────────────────┼────────────────────────
     ┤
     │                      │                          │ "SMS provider'ına
     │
     │ provider_unreachable │ satır yeni ve circuit ∈  │ ulaşılamıyor,
     │
     │                      │ {open, half_open}        │ bekleniyor" (+
     │
     │                      │                          │ last_error)
     │
     ├──────────────────────┼──────────────────────────┼────────────────────────
     ┤
     │                      │ satır yok ya da          │
     │
     │ worker_down          │ age_seconds >            │ "Worker çalışmıyor"
     │
     │                      │ WORKER_STALE_AFTER       │
     │
     └──────────────────────┴──────────────────────────┴────────────────────────
     ┘

     - Eşik WORKER_STALE_AFTER (api ayarı, varsayılan 10 s, heartbeat aralığının
       5 katı). Yaş DB saatiyle hesaplanır, api ile worker saatleri arasındaki
       fark etkilemez.
     - worker_down, provider_unreachable'a baskındır: eski bir satırın open
       demesi güncel bilgi sayılmaz.
     - Sorgu: stats sorgusu + ChannelStatuses (2 satırlık tablo); her ikisi
       hafiftir, 1 sn'lik polling'i taşır.

     Metrikler

     - api /metrics:
       - scc_http_request_duration_seconds{method,route,status}
       - scc_llm_calls_total{task,outcome}, scc_llm_call_duration_seconds{task}
       - scc_deliveries{channel,status}: scrape anında DB'den grup sayımı; hata
         durumunda scrape hatası olarak görünür.
     - Worker metrikleri: worker kendi /metrics ve /healthz uçlarını
       WORKER_METRICS_ADDR (varsayılan :9091) üzerinde sunar.
       - scc_worker_attempts_total{channel,result}
       - scc_worker_circuit_state{channel}
       - scc_worker_channel_lock_held{channel}
       - Worker prometheus import etmez. Bir Observer arayüzü üzerinden çalışır,
         prometheus uygulaması internal/metrics paketindedir. Seçilen yol
         DECISIONS'a yazılır.

     Komutlar ve compose

     - composer api: API_ADDR (:8080), LLM_MODE, LLM_MAX_CONCURRENCY.
       - SIGTERM'de: RegisterOnShutdown stream context'ini iptal eder. Açık SSE
         akışları error {code: server_shutting_down} gönderip kapanır, ardından
         Shutdown(15s) çalışır.
       - ReadHeaderTimeout 5 s. WriteTimeout yok (SSE için).
     - docker-compose.yml:
       - api ortamına WORKER_STALE_AFTER: ${WORKER_STALE_AFTER:-10s}, worker'a
         WORKER_HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL: ${WORKER_HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL:-2s}.
       - api servisi: seed tamamlanınca başlar; port 127.0.0.1:8080:8080 (auth
         olmadığı ve kredi harcayabildiği için yalnızca localhost); healthcheck
         wget /readyz; stop_grace_period: 20s.
       - Ortam: LLM_MODE: ${LLM_MODE:-auto}, ANTHROPIC_API_KEY: 
         ${ANTHROPIC_API_KEY:-}, ANTHROPIC_MODEL. .env yoksa boş kalır ve mock
         çalışır.
       - Worker'a healthcheck ve 127.0.0.1:9091 portu eklenir.
     - .env.example'a LLM_MODE ve LLM_MAX_CONCURRENCY yorum satırı olarak
       eklenir.

     Testler (hepsi make test içinde, key gerektirmez)

     - Unit: durum makinesi tablo testi, Warnings, hata eşlemesi, breaker,
       retry, FromEnv.
     - Handler (integration tag, testdb + httptest + llm.Scripted):
       - geçerli akış
       - bozuk JSON çıktısı ve onarımın tükenmesi (502 + problems)
       - refusal (422)
       - 429 (Retry-After: 30 başlığı)
       - yanlış durum (409)
       - version uyuşmazlığı (409)
       - ikinci onay (409)
       - onaydan sonra düzenleme, yeniden üretme ve kanal değişikliği (409)
       - içerik doğrulama hataları (alan yolları)
       - brief limiti / 413 / bilinmeyen alan
       - match-all uyarısı ve teyit olmadan onay (422), teyitle onay (200)
     - SSE:
       - event sırası (attempt, delta'lar, done)
       - onarımda ikinci attempt
       - hata event'i
       - istemci kapanınca iptal: test içi, ctx'e kadar bloklayan bir client ile
         LLM ctx'inin iptal edildiği gözlenir
       - eşzamanlı ikinci üretim 409
       - content_ready'de ?regenerate=true olmadan istek (EventSource'un 
         otomatik yeniden bağlanması): 409 content_exists, Scripted client'a hiç
         istek gitmez (Requests() boş), içerik ve version değişmez; 
         regenerate=true ile aynı istek akar ve içerik yenilenir; regenerate=abc
         422
       - stream sürerken araya giren PUT /content: stream state_changed ile
         biter, elle yapılan düzenleme veritabanında kalır
       - stats: worker_channel_status satırı yeni + closed → ok; open →
         provider_unreachable (+ last_error); updated_at eşiğin ötesine
         çekilince → worker_down; satır yokken → worker_down
       - shutdown'da server_shutting_down
     - E2E (integration): seed.Run(500), demo mock, in-process mockprovider ve
       worker.
       POST → SSE → approve → /stats completed. deliveries sayısı = POST'taki
       sms + email izinli sayısı = bağımsız el yazımı SQL sayımı.

      352 +// silentProvider reads each request and closes the connection
          + without a reply, like a provider that dies mid-request.      
      353 +// It records the requests per idempotency key and when they a
          +rrived.                                                       
      354 +type silentProvider struct {                                  
      355 +  srv      *httptest.Server                                   
      356 +  mu       sync.Mutex                                         
      357 +  keys     map[string]int                                     
      358 +  arrivals []time.Time                                        
      359 +}                                                             
      360 +                                                              
      361 +func newSilentProvider(t *testing.T) *silentProvider {        
      362 +  t.Helper()                                                  
      363 +  p := &silentProvider{keys: map[string]int{}}                
      364 +  p.srv = httptest.NewServer(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.Resp
          +onseWriter, r *http.Request) {                                
      365 +    _, _ = io.Copy(io.Discard, r.Body)                        
      366 +    p.mu.Lock()                                               
      367 +    p.keys[r.Header.Get("Idempotency-Key")]++                 
      368 +    p.arrivals = append(p.arrivals, time.Now())               
      369 +    p.mu.Unlock()                                             
      370 +    conn, _, err := w.(http.Hijacker).Hijack()                
      371 +    if err != nil {                                           
      372 +      t.Errorf("hijacking the connection: %v", err)           
      373 +      return                                                  
      374 +    }                                                         
      375 +    _ = conn.Close()                                          
      376 +  }))                                                         
      377 +  t.Cleanup(p.srv.Close)                                      
      378 +  return p                                                    
      379 +}                                                             
      380 +                                                              
      381 +// requestsPerKey copies the per-key request counts.          
      382 +func (p *silentProvider) requestsPerKey() map[string]int {    
      383 +  p.mu.Lock()                                                 
      384 +  defer p.mu.Unlock()                                         
      385 +  return maps.Clone(p.keys)                                   
      386 +}                                                             
      387 +                                                              
      388 +// maxPerSecond is the largest number of requests that arrived
          + in any half-open second [t, t+1s).                           
      389 +func (p *silentProvider) maxPerSecond() int {                 
      390 +  p.mu.Lock()                                                 
      391 +  defer p.mu.Unlock()                                         
      392 +  best, lo := 0, 0                                            
      393 +  for hi, at := range p.arrivals {                            
      394 +    for at.Sub(p.arrivals[lo]) >= time.Second {               
      395 +      lo++                                                    
      396 +    }                                                         
      397 +    best = max(best, hi-lo+1)                                 
      398 +  }                                                           
      399 +  return best                                                 
      400 +}                                                             
      401 +                                                              
      402 +// The fifth attempt and the confirmation request both get no 
          +answer at all: the delivery fails, its last_error says        
      403 +// that the outcome is unknown (not just "gave up"), the provi
          +der saw exactly two requests with the same key, and the       
      404 +// confirmation request went through the rate limiter like eve
          +ry other send.

      325  - **Decision:** `retry.Policy.Decide(attempt, result, jitter)`
            returns done, retry-after-delay or fail. Backoff is
      326    `1s * 2^(n-1)` capped at 1 minute with "equal jitter" (half 
           fixed, half random). A 429 waits at least its
      327    Retry-After (capped at 5 minutes) or the backoff, whichever 
           is longer. 5xx, 408, timeouts and connection errors are
      328 -  retried; every other 4xx is permanent; at most 5 attempts. `
          -Retry-After` is read as seconds or HTTP date.                 
      329 -  **One exception, found by `make soak`:** if the fifth attemp
          -t ends without any answer (timeout or connection              
      330 -  error), the delivery gets one more attempt with the same key
          - instead of being failed, so a delivery can use               
      331 -  6 attempts, never more (`Policy.MaxTotalAttempts`, `confirms
          -`). A lease that expires on the fifth attempt is              
      332 -  treated the same way.                                       
      328 +  retried; every other 4xx is permanent; at most 5 send attemp
          +ts, plus the confirmation request described in the next       
      329 +  entry. `Retry-After` is read as seconds or HTTP date.       
      330  - **Why:** with the clock and the jitter passed in, every rule
            is a table test with exact numbers. 408 is
      331    listed with the timeouts on purpose: it is a timeout, and th
           e case says timeouts are retried.
      335 -  The exception: the first soak run had a delivery whose fifth
          - attempt hit a late reply. The provider had accepted the      
      336 -  message, the worker timed out, wrote `failed ("gave up after
          - 5 attempts: timeout")` and the database disagreed with       
      337 -  the provider by one message. A timeout is not a "no", it is 
          -"unknown", and failing it is wrong whenever the message       
      338 -  was delivered. The confirmation attempt cannot deliver twice
          - (same key): if the provider has the message it               
      339 -  replies with the first answer, otherwise it is a normal last
          - try. A 5xx or 429 on the fifth attempt is an answer          
      340 -  ("not accepted") and still fails the delivery.              
      341 -- **Alternative:** keep the hard limit of 5 and accept that a 
          -delivery whose last attempt timed out may be recorded as      
      342 -  failed although the customer got the message (an invariant "
          -provider accepted = database sent" that cannot hold),         
      343 -  or a separate `unknown` status. This deviates from the liter
          -al "at most 5 attempts"; it is the one place to review        
      344 -  if the stricter reading is wanted (change `confirms` to retu
          -rn false).                                                    
      332  - **Alternative:** full jitter (can retry almost at once, whic
           h defeats the backoff).
      333  
      334 +### At most 5 send attempts; if the outcome is unknown, 1 conf
          +irmation request with the same idempotency key                
      335 +- **Decision:** a delivery gets at most 5 send attempts. If th
          +e fifth ends without any answer (timeout or connection        
      336 +  error), one more request is made with the same idempotency k
          +ey: the confirmation request (`Policy.confirms`,              
      337 +  `MaxTotalAttempts` = 6). It is an ordinary claim, so it wait
          +s for a rate-limit token like every other send (an            
      338 +  integration test sends 300 requests through a provider that 
          +never answers and checks the busiest second). If it also      
      339 +  gets no answer, the delivery becomes `failed` and `last_erro
          +r` says so: `outcome unknown: attempt 5 and the               
      340 +  confirmation request with the same idempotency key (attempt 
          +6) both ended without an answer (timeout); the provider       
      341 +  may have delivered the message`. A lease that expires on the
          + last attempt (a worker killed mid-send) is the same          
      342 +  case and gets the same wording (`RecoverLeases`). A 5xx or 4
          +29 on the fifth attempt is an answer ("not accepted") and     
      343 +  fails the delivery as `gave up after N attempts`, without a 
          +confirmation.                                                 
      344 +- **Why:** found by `make soak`: a fifth attempt hit a late re

      345 +  wrote `failed ("gave up after 5 attempts: timeout")` and the
          + database disagreed with the provider by one message. A       
      346 +  timeout is not a "no", it is "unknown", and failing it as a 
          +plain failure is wrong whenever the message was               
      347 +  delivered. The confirmation cannot deliver twice (same key):

      348 +  first answer, otherwise it is a normal last try. When even t
          +hat gets no answer there is nothing left to ask, so the       
      349 +  delivery fails and the text says plainly that the result is 
          +unknown instead of implying a clean failure.                  
      350 +- **Alternative:** keep the hard limit of 5 and record such a 
          +delivery as a plain failure (the invariant "provider accepted 
          +=                                                             
      351 +  database sent" could not hold), or a separate `unknown` stat
          +us (one more state in every query and in the dashboard        
      352 +  for a rare case; the text in `last_error` carries the inform
          +ation). This deviates from the literal "at most 5             
      353 +  attempts" by one request that cannot send twice; `confirms` 
          +returning false restores the strict reading.

● Update(DECISIONS.md)
  ⎿  Removed 3 lines
      477  - **The idempotency key is the bare delivery id.** If the data
           base is restored or reset while a long-lived provider keeps
      478    its memory, ids repeat and new messages are treated as dupli
           cates (and recorded as `sent`). Adding the campaign id to
      479    the key costs nothing but changes every test that expects ke
           y = id; left for a quiet moment.
      480 -- **A lease that expires on the confirmation attempt (a worker
          - killed mid-send) fails the delivery**, although the provider 
      481 -  may have accepted the message. Accepted: there is no further
          - information to confirm with. The `soak` would show           
      482 -  it as a key difference.                                     
      480  - **The retry window is short** (five attempts span about 7-15
            s plus request time). A provider outage longer than that, or
      481    a wrong `PROVIDER_URL` (not validated at start-up; a URL wit
           hout a scheme is classed transient), sends every delivery to
      482    `failed` for good. A circuit breaker that parks `queued` row
           s while the provider is down would be the real fix.

      310    long) must not stall the pacing.
      311  - **Alternative:** claim batches of 50 and pace inside the wor
           ker; faster per row, but stale consent and long leases.
      312  
      313 -### The idempotency key is the delivery id and results are fen
          -ced by the attempt number                                     
      314 -- **Decision:** the provider key is the delivery id, identical
          - on every attempt and after a lease recovery. `attempts`      
      315 -  is incremented by the claim and every result is written with
          - `WHERE status = 'sending' AND attempts = <mine>`;            
      316 -  a result that no longer matches (the lease expired and the r
          -ow moved on) is dropped (`ErrLeaseLost`).                     
      313 +### The idempotency key is a UUID made at enqueue, and results
          + are fenced by the attempt number                             
      314 +- **Decision:** `deliveries.idempotency_key` (UUID, unique, `D
          +EFAULT gen_random_uuid()`, migration 0003) is filled when     
      315 +  the delivery is enqueued, and that value is the provider's i
          +dempotency key: identical on every attempt, on the            
      316 +  confirmation request and after a lease recovery. `attempts` 
          +is incremented by the claim and every result is written       
      317 +  with `WHERE status = 'sending' AND attempts = <mine>`; a res
          +ult that no longer matches (the lease expired and the row     
      318 +  moved on) is dropped (`ErrLeaseLost`).                      
      319  - **Why:** this is what makes "timed out but delivered" safe: 
           the retry carries the same key and the provider
      320    answers with the first reply. The fence stops a slow worker 
           from overwriting the result of the attempt that
      319 -  replaced it. The tests prove it key by key: the set of keys 
          -the provider accepted equals the set of delivery ids          
      320 -  the database calls `sent`, with exact timeouts, 500s, 429s a
          -nd late replies in play.                                      
      321 -- **Alternative:** a fresh key per attempt (simpler, double se
          -nds after any timeout), or locking the row while              
      322 -  the HTTP call runs (holds a connection per in-flight send). 
      321 +  replaced it. The tests prove it key by key: the set of keys 
          +the provider accepted equals the idempotency keys of the      
      322 +  deliveries the database calls `sent`, with exact timeouts, 5
          +00s, 429s and late replies in play.                           
      323 +  The key used to be the bare delivery id. If the database is 
          +reset (or restored) while a long-lived provider keeps its     
      324 +  memory, ids start from 1 again, new messages look like dupli
          +cates and are recorded as `sent` without being sent; a        
      325 +  UUID cannot repeat. A test runs two databases whose ids both
          + start at 1 against one provider and requires zero            
      326 +  duplicates. It is generated by the database, so no UUID libr
          +ary is needed and the value exists in the same statement      
      327 +  that creates the row.                                       
      328 +- **Alternative:** a fresh key per attempt (simpler, double se
          +nds after any timeout), locking the row while the HTTP        
      329 +  call runs (holds a connection per in-flight send), or `campa
          +ign_id-delivery_id` (still repeats after a reset).

● Update(DECISIONS.md)
  ⎿  Removed 3 lines
      481  ### Worker, queue and operations
      482  - **The rate cap is per worker process.** Two workers allow tw
           ice the cap at the provider. PLAN assumption 9 says one
      483    instance; a shared limiter is out of scope. The compose file
            runs exactly one worker.
      484 -- **The idempotency key is the bare delivery id.** If the data
          -base is restored or reset while a long-lived provider keeps   
      485 -  its memory, ids repeat and new messages are treated as dupli
          -cates (and recorded as `sent`). Adding the campaign id to     
      486 -  the key costs nothing but changes every test that expects ke
          -y = id; left for a quiet moment.                              
      484  - **The retry window is short** (five attempts span about 7-15
            s plus request time). A provider outage longer than that, or
      485    a wrong `PROVIDER_URL` (not validated at start-up; a URL wit
           hout a scheme is classed transient), sends every delivery to
      486    `failed` for good. A circuit breaker that parks `queued` row
           s while the provider is down would be the real fix.

● Update(DECISIONS.md)
  ⎿  Added 20 lines
      371  - **Alternative:** the token bucket alone at exactly 100/s. It
            matches the wording of the requirement but fails the
      372    measurement. Setting it to 95/s works too, but then nothing 
           states why 95.
      373  
      374 +### Each channel is served by the worker that holds its adviso
          +ry lock                                                       
      375 +- **Decision:** before it claims anything, a worker takes `pg_
          +try_advisory_lock(0x5343, channel)` for each channel (sms = 1,
      376 +  email = 2; the two-integer form, so it cannot collide with t
          +he migration and seed locks, which use the one-bigint         
      377 +  form). A worker that does not get the lock waits and tries a
          +gain every `LockRetryInterval` (5 s by default) and logs      
      378 +  once per wait. The lock is session-level, so the dispatcher 
          +holds one dedicated connection per channel and makes its      
      379 +  claims through it (`queue.ChannelLock`). If a claim fails, t
          +he dispatcher gives the lock up (an unlock that fails closes  
      380 +  the session instead of returning it to the pool, as in `migr
          +ate`) and takes it again from scratch. The rate limiter       
      381 +  and the send slots live outside one tenure of the lock. Leas
          +e recovery and campaign completion run in every worker; they  
      382 +  are idempotent.                                             
      383 +- **Why:** the 100 messages per second cap is enforced inside 
          +one process, so a second worker would double it               
      384 +  (assumption 9 in docs/PLAN.md). With the lock, any number of
          + workers can run and only one sends per channel; the others   
      385 +  are standbys that take over within `LockRetryInterval` of th
          +e holder stopping or dying (the server drops the lock when    
      386 +  the session ends; a test kills the session and checks that a
          + claim over it fails and another worker gets the lock). A     
      387 +  test with two workers measures the busiest second at the pro
          +vider across the hand-over (at most 100 per channel, no       
      388 +  duplicate keys). A holder whose session dies keeps sending u
          +ntil its next claim, which is at most one poll away and       
      389 +  fails; during that moment another worker may hold the lock t
          +oo, so the cap can be exceeded for the length of one claim.   
      390 +- **Alternative:** a shared token bucket in the database (a ro
          +und trip per message), or leader election by a heartbeat      
      391 +  table (more moving parts than the lock the database already 
          +offers). Scaling throughput with more workers is not a goal:  
      392 +  the cap is per channel, so the standby only provides availab
          +ility.                                                        
      393 +                                                              
      394  ### Lease recovery, completion and shutdown
      395  - **Decision:** a maintenance loop (every second) returns deli
           veries whose lease expired to `queued`, due at once,
      396    without resetting `attempts`; one whose attempts are used up
            becomes `failed`. The same loop completes campaigns

      131 +// validateProviderURL rejects a provider address that cannot 
          +work (no scheme, no host, another scheme). Without this a     
      132 +// typo such as "mock-provider:8081" is taken for an unknown s
          +cheme at the first request, which is classed as a             
      133 +// transient error, and every delivery would be retried for no
          +thing. The value is not echoed: it can carry credentials.     
      134 +func validateProviderURL(raw string) error {                  
      135 +  u, err := url.Parse(raw)                                    
      136 +  if err != nil || (u.Scheme != "http" && u.Scheme != "https")
          + || u.Hostname() == "" {                                      
      137 +    return fmt.Errorf("%s must be an http or https URL with a 
          +host, for example http://mock-provider:8081", envProviderURL) 
      138 +  }                                                           
      139 +  return nil                                                  
      140 +}                                                             
      141 +

● Update(DECISIONS.md)
  ⎿  Added 2 lines, removed 1 line
      330  
      331  ### Retry policy is a pure function
      332  - **Decision:** `retry.Policy.Decide(attempt, result, jitter)`
            returns done, retry-after-delay or fail. Backoff is
      333 -  `1s * 2^(n-1)` capped at 1 minute with "equal jitter" (half 
          -fixed, half random). A 429 waits at least its                 
      333 +  `15s * 2^(n-1)` capped at 2 minutes with "equal jitter" (hal
          +f fixed, half random): the four waits between five            
      334 +  attempts add up to 112-225 s, a retry window of two to four 
          +minutes. A 429 waits at least its                             
      335    Retry-After (capped at 5 minutes) or the backoff, whichever 
           is longer. 5xx, 408, timeouts and connection errors are
      336    retried; every other 4xx is permanent; at most 5 send attemp
           ts, plus the confirmation request described in the next
      337    entry. `Retry-After` is read as seconds or HTTP date.

      502  ### Worker, queue and operations
      503 -- **The retry window is short** (five attempts span about 7-15
          - s plus request time). A provider outage longer than that, or 
      504 -  a wrong `PROVIDER_URL` (not validated at start-up; a URL wit
          -hout a scheme is classed transient), sends every delivery to  
      505 -  `failed` for good. A circuit breaker that parks `queued` row
          -s while the provider is down would be the real fix.           
      503  - `composer api` is still a stub that exits 0, which a restart
            policy or healthcheck would read as a finished healthy
      504    service. It should exit non-zero until implemented (nothing 
           in compose runs it yet).

● Update(DECISIONS.md)
  ⎿  Added 38 lines
      372  - **Alternative:** the token bucket alone at exactly 100/s. It
            matches the wording of the requirement but fails the
      373    measurement. Setting it to 95/s works too, but then nothing 
           states why 95.
      374  
      375 +### A circuit breaker per channel parks the queue during an ou
          +tage and gives the attempts back                              
      376 +- **Decision:** each channel has a breaker (`worker/breaker.go
          +`, a pure state machine with an injected clock). Ten requests 
          +in                                                            
      377 +  a row that end without an answer or with a 5xx (`Threshold`)
          + open it. While it is open the dispatcher claims nothing.     
      378 +  After the cooldown (10 s) exactly one request, the probe, is
          + let through (half-open): an answer, even a 429 or a 4xx, clos
          +es                                                            
      379 +  the circuit; a failure opens it again and the cooldown start
          +s over. A 429 is neutral while closed (it neither adds to nor 
      380 +  resets the run). **Attempts are given back:** a failure that
          + comes back while the circuit is not closed (a request that   
      381 +  was in flight when it opened, or the probe) does not count: 
          +`Finish` queues the delivery again with `attempts - 1`        
      382 +  (`Outcome.Refund`; the fence on the attempt number still app
          +lies). The failure that itself opens the circuit counts, so an
      383 +  outage burns at most `Threshold` attempts per channel. A pro
          +be permit that is not used (nothing due, consent withdrawn,   
      384 +  claim error) is handed back (`Cancel`), or the half-open sta
          +te would wait forever. Because the claiming stops and the rest
      385 +  is refunded, an outage of any length fails no delivery; the 
          +campaign stays `sending` until the provider answers again.    
      386 +  `Breaker.Disabled` exists for the tests about giving up, whe
          +re a provider that fails everything must exhaust attempts.    
      387 +- **Retry window:** the default backoff is now 15 s doubling t
          +o 2 min (see "Retry policy"), so the retries alone cover an   
      388 +  outage of two to four minutes and the breaker covers anythin
          +g longer. Start-up now rejects a `PROVIDER_URL` without an    
      389 +  http(s) scheme and host (it used to be classed as a transien
          +t error and retried for nothing); the value is not echoed.    
      390 +- **Why:** with the old 7-15 s of retries, a provider restart 
          +sent every delivery to `failed` for good. Counting only the   
      391 +  failures seen while the circuit was still closed keeps the a
          +ttempt limit meaningful (a genuinely bad message still fails  
      392 +  after five attempts) without letting an outage use it up. A 
          +20-second outage test (10 s of dropped connections, then 10 s 
      393 +  of 503) runs with a fast backoff and a 300 ms cooldown: noth
          +ing may fail and the provider must see only a few probes; the 
      394 +  same test with the breaker off fails, which is how it was ch
          +ecked.                                                        
      395 +- **Alternative:** a longer backoff alone (every delivery stil
          +l fails after the window, and 5,000 deliveries would hammer a 
      396 +  recovering provider at once), or counting the refunded failu
          +res (the pre-change behaviour).                               
      397 +- **Known limits:** the circuit is per worker process; with tw
          +o workers the standby starts with a closed circuit when it tak
          +es a                                                          
      398 +  channel over. A provider that is up but answers every reques
          +t with a 5xx for a different reason (bad credentials would be 
          +a                                                             
      399 +  4xx) keeps the circuit open and the campaign `sending`, whic
          +h the dashboard shows as "provider unreachable".              
      400 +                                                              
      401 +### The worker reports its channels in `worker_channel_status`
      402 +- **Decision:** the worker that serves a channel (the holder o
          +f the lock) writes `(channel, circuit, last_error, updated_at)
          +`                                                             
      403 +  every 2 s (`WORKER_HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL`) and at once when the
          + circuit changes, from a goroutine of its own so a busy send l
          +oop                                                           
      404 +  cannot stall it. `updated_at` and the age come from the data
          +base clock. `last_error` is the short text of the last failure
      405 +  while the circuit is not closed, empty otherwise. A standby 
          +worker writes nothing. A failed write is logged once per strea
          +k                                                             
      406 +  and counted; it never stops the sending.                    
      407 +- **Why:** the dashboard has to tell "the provider is unreacha
          +ble, waiting" (a fresh row with an open circuit) from "the wor
          +ker                                                           
      408 +  is not running" (an old or missing row). A shared table need
          +s no extra port between the API and the worker, and the age is
      409 +  measured in one place. The API turns this into `provider.sta
          +te` in the stats (next section).                              
      410 +- **Alternative:** the API asks the worker over HTTP (a second
          + service dependency, and no answer when the worker is down is 
      411 +  ambiguous), or Prometheus gauges only (not available to the 
          +UI without a scrape).                                         
      412 +                                                              
      413  ### Each channel is served by the worker that holds its adviso
           ry lock
      414  - **Decision:** before it claims anything, a worker takes `pg_
           try_advisory_lock(0x5343, channel)` for each channel (sms = 1,
      415    email = 2; the two-integer form, so it cannot collide with t
           he migration and seed locks, which use the one-bigint

● Update(DECISIONS.md)
  ⎿  Added 31 lines
      448  - **Why:** the audience is the one the marketer saw when appro
           ving; running the worker later or twice cannot change it.
      449  - **Alternative:** compiling with `now()` at enqueue time (the
            audience drifts by the hours between approval and enqueue).
      450  
      451 +## Campaign API                                               
      452 +                                                              
      453 +### A segment that matches everyone, or a brief that could not
          + be expressed, is flagged and needs an acknowledgement        
      454 +- **Decision:** `campaign.Warnings(audience, unsupported)` is 
          +a pure function that returns `unsupported_criteria` (the      
      455 +  non-blank criteria the model listed, as `details`) and `matc
          +hes_everyone` (`matched == total_users > 0`). The API puts the
      456 +  list in the campaign's `warnings` field, and approval needs 
          +`acknowledge_warnings: true` when the list is not empty       
      457 +  (`campaign.RequireAcknowledgement`, 422 `acknowledgement_req
          +uired` otherwise). The segment prompt now says                
      458 +  "never invent a condition for a criterion you cannot express
          +, never guess a city, language, amount or time window the     
      459 +  brief does not state, put it in `unsupported_criteria`", and
          + that the catch-all rule is for a brief with nothing          
      460 +  expressible (`unsupported_criteria` empty only when the brie
          +f simply asks for all customers). The demo mock has both      
      461 +  scenarios: nothing recognised (catch-all rule plus an unsupp
          +orted criterion) and an explicit "tüm müşteriler" (catch-all, 
          +no                                                            
      462 +  unsupported criterion).                                     
      463 +- **Why:** the model has to put some condition in the rule, so
          + a brief with nothing expressible produced a match-all rule an
          +d                                                             
      464 +  the only signal was a list the UI might not show. The count 
          +is the check that cannot be talked around: the model's own    
      465 +  `unsupported_criteria` is advisory, whereas "the rule reache
          +s every user" is a fact read from the database. An explicit   
      466 +  acknowledgement turns "I did not notice" into a decision, an
          +d the broadcast case is flagged even when the brief asked for 
          +it                                                            
      467 +  because sending to everyone should be a deliberate click. No
          + users at all is not "everyone".                              
      468 +- **Alternative:** reject the match-all shape in `compose` (it
          + cannot tell a deliberate broadcast from a failure, and needs 
          +the                                                           
      469 +  database to know "everyone"), or only warn without asking fo
          +r an acknowledgement (what happened before: it passed silently
          +).                                                            
      470 +                                                              
      471 +### The brief names its channels in the rule; empty means both
      472 +- **Decision:** the segment schema and `segment.Rule` have a `
          +channels` array (`sms`, `email`), required in the schema and  
      473 +  optional in `Decode` (absent = the brief names none, the sam
          +e as an empty array), validated for unknown values and        
      474 +  repeats (`channels[i]` paths, shared by `Validate` and `Comp
          +ile`). It never reaches the SQL. The campaign opens with the  
      475 +  named channels, or both when the list is empty (PLAN assumpt
          +ion 4); the marketer can still change them before approval. Th
          +e                                                             
      476 +  schema drift test compares the channel list with the schema 
          +in both directions.                                           
      477 +- **Why:** reading "SMS only" out of free text with keywords i
          +n Go would misread "no SMS, please" and would leave the model 
      478 +  out of a decision it is good at. The vocabulary is shared, s
          +o a new channel cannot be added to one side only.             
      479 +- **Alternative:** keyword matching in Go (rejected above), or
          + a channel selector in the UI only (the brief would have to be
      480 +  read twice, and "email campaign" would still open an SMS cha
          +nnel).                                                        
      481 +                                                              
      482  ## Docker and the test commands
      483  
      484  ### `make test` is a compose file of its own, and the hook tes
           ts skip Windows drive paths on Linux

● Update(DECISIONS.md)

      545  "Fixed after the review" above.)
      546  
      547  ### LLM layer (internal/llm, internal/compose)
      548 -- **A brief with nothing expressible becomes a match-all rule.
          -** The prompt tells the model to emit `language in [tr, en]`  
      549 -  when nothing can be said, and the validator accepts it (mock
          - mode does the same). The only signal is                      
      550 -  `unsupported_criteria`. Needs a product decision before the 
          -API exists: a flag on `SegmentResult`                         
      551 -  (for example `MatchesEveryone`), or a compose-level rejectio
          -n of that shape, and an approval step that requires the       
      552 -  marketer to acknowledge a non-empty `unsupported_criteria`. 
          -(Severity: high, design.)                                     
      548  - **Without a key the system silently serves the demo mock** (
           `llm.FromEnv`). It is the documented behaviour (PLAN
      549    section 1, "Mock LLM" badge), but a key that fails to load l
           ooks like a working system. When the API is wired: log a
      550    warning at start-up and consider an explicit `LLM_MODE=mock`
            opt-in.

● Update(DECISIONS.md)
  ⎿  Removed 3 lines
      544  "Fixed after the review" above.)
      545  
      546  ### LLM layer (internal/llm, internal/compose)
      547 -- **Without a key the system silently serves the demo mock** (
          -`llm.FromEnv`). It is the documented behaviour (PLAN          
      548 -  section 1, "Mock LLM" badge), but a key that fails to load l
          -ooks like a working system. When the API is wired: log a      
      549 -  warning at start-up and consider an explicit `LLM_MODE=mock`
          - opt-in.                                                      
      547  - **`compose.Content` emits no terminal event and calls `onEve
           nt` inside the SDK read loop.** After a final error the text
      548    already streamed stays on screen, and a blocking SSE writer 
           stalls the provider read. The API layer must send a
      549    failure/discard event and keep `onEvent` non-blocking (buffe
           r, cancel on overflow).

● Update(DECISIONS.md)
  ⎿  Added 13 lines
       96  - **Alternative:** raw HTTP. More code to own and no gain. A f
           loating SDK version was rejected: a `go get -u`
       97    could silently change request shapes.
       98  
       99 +### LLM_MODE: auto, anthropic or mock, and a missing key is an
          + error when the real model was asked for                      
      100 +- **Decision:** `LLM_MODE` is `auto` (default), `anthropic` or
          + `mock`. `auto` uses the real client when `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` 
          +is set                                                        
      101 +  and the demo mock otherwise (so `docker compose up` works wi
          +thout a dotenv file). `anthropic` without a key makes the     
      102 +  api exit at start-up with an error (`ErrNoAPIKey`) instead o
          +f falling back. `mock` never calls the real API, even if a    
      103 +  key is set (the log line says the key is ignored). Any other
          + value is an error that does not echo the value, in case a    
      104 +  key was pasted into the wrong variable. `FromEnv` returns a 
          +`Selection` (client, setting and a one-sentence reason without
      105 +  the key); the api logs mode, model and reason at start-up an
          +d `/api/config` returns `llm_mode` for the badge.             
      106 +- **Why:** a key that failed to load used to look like a worki
          +ng system, because the fallback to the mock was silent. Someon
          +e who                                                         
      107 +  sets `LLM_MODE=anthropic` has said what they want, so a miss
          +ing key must be loud; someone who sets nothing gets the demo. 
      108 +  `mock` is the opt-in for demos and for recording a run while
          + a key is present on the machine.                             
      109 +- **Alternative:** warn and fall back to the mock in every cas
          +e (still looks like a working system to a script), or fail wit
          +hout a key                                                    
      110 +  in every case (breaks `docker compose up` for someone withou
          +t one, which the case requires to work).                      
      111 +                                                              
      112  ### One structured-output request per task, effort set explici
           tly, nothing the model rejects
      113  - **Decision:** segment: one non-streaming request, `output_co
           nfig.format` = the schema, effort `low`.
      114    Content: streaming, same mechanism with the content schema, 
           effort `medium`. Thinking is left unset

● Container'daki Postgres VACUUM sonrası görünürlük haritasını işaretlemediği
  için varsayılan plan Bitmap Index Scan oluyor; bunu iki adımda doğrulayacağım:
  önce gerçek planın yeni index'i kullanıp Seq Scan içermediğine, sonra
  enable_bitmapscan=off ile Index Only Scan'in çıkıp çıkmadığına bakacağım.
