● Update(DECISIONS.md)  ⎿  Added 114 lines
      491  - **Alternative:** keyword matching in Go (rejected above), or
            a channel selector in the UI only (the brief would have to be
      492    read twice, and "email campaign" would still open an SMS cha
           nnel).
      493  
      494 +### The state machine is a pure function; every change is a co
          +nditional UPDATE; the content survives a new segment          
      495 +- **Decision:** `campaign.Next(state, action)` is a table (`Ge
          +nerateSegment`, `SetChannels`, `SaveContent`, `Approve`) and a
      496 +  test walks every state against every action. A new segment i
          +s allowed in draft, audience_ready and content_ready, and keep
          +s                                                             
      497 +  the state it was in: a campaign with content stays `content_
          +ready` (the text is not thrown away, but the version moves, so
      498 +  the approval must name the new one). Channels are set from t
          +he brief when the campaign leaves draft and belong to the     
      499 +  marketer afterwards. `approved` exists only inside the appro
          +val transaction; the same transaction moves the campaign to   
      500 +  `sending` (or to `completed` when nobody is eligible), so th
          +e worker and the dashboard never see it. Every write is       
      501 +  `UPDATE ... WHERE id = $1 AND status = <read> AND version = 
          +<read>` (`ErrStateChanged` when it matches no row), and the   
      502 +  fragments that assign columns are constants of the package, 
          +never request data.                                           
      503 +- **Why:** the check and the write cannot be separated by a co
          +ncurrent request, and an edit computed from stale data cannot 
      504 +  overwrite newer work. The API checks the state first and the
          + values second, so an edit after approval is a 409 whatever it
          +s                                                             
      505 +  body says.                                                  
      506 +- **Alternative:** a trigger or a lock held while the model ru
          +ns (hidden logic, or a connection per generation).            
      507 +                                                              
      508 +### Approval: version, one transaction, a snapshot the worker 
          +reads                                                         
      509 +- **Decision:** the approve request must send the `version` th
          +e marketer reviewed (422 without it, 409 `version_mismatch` wi
          +th                                                            
      510 +  the current version when it differs). `Store.Approve` locks 
          +the row (`FOR UPDATE`), checks the state (so the second approv
          +al                                                            
      511 +  is a 409 `invalid_state`), checks the version, decodes and v
          +alidates the stored rule and content again, counts the        
      512 +  audience at the transaction's `now()` (the instant `approved
          +_at` gets, so the count is the audience the queue selects),   
      513 +  requires `acknowledge_warnings` when there are warnings, cop
          +ies rule, content, channels and the warnings into             
      514 +  `approved_rule`, `approved_content`, `approved_channels` and
          + `approved_warnings`, and calls `queue.EnqueueTx` for the     
      515 +  recipients. `Enqueue` and `Claim` read only the snapshot col
          +umns. Checks in the schema keep a campaign from being approved
      516 +  without a complete snapshot.                                
      517 +- **Why:** content that was reviewed is the content that is se
          +nt, even if the editable columns are changed afterwards (a tes
          +t                                                             
      518 +  changes `content` after the approval and the worker still ge
          +ts the approved text). Eight concurrent approvals of one campa
          +ign                                                           
      519 +  queue the recipients once (tested). The rule is stored as th
          +e validated model JSON; `jsonb` makes duplicate keys last-wins
          +,                                                             
      520 +  the same as `segment.Decode`, so the stored text and the val
          +idated value agree.                                           
      521 +- **Alternative:** approve by id only (a campaign edited betwe
          +en review and click is approved unseen), or snapshot into a   
      522 +  separate table (a second place to keep consistent).         
      523 +                                                              
      524 +### One error shape; codes are the contract                   
      525 +- **Decision:** every error response, including 404 and 405, i
          +s `{"error": {"code", "message", "details"}}`. Codes used:    
      526 +  `invalid_json` 400, `validation_failed` 422 (details: `[{pat
          +h, message}]`), `acknowledgement_required` 422 (details: the  
      527 +  warnings), `not_found` 404 (also a malformed id), `invalid_s
          +tate`, `state_changed`, `version_mismatch`, `content_exists`, 
      528 +  `generation_in_progress` 409, `body_too_large` 413, `llm_ref
          +used` 422, `llm_rate_limited` 429 (with `Retry-After` and     
      529 +  `details.retry_after_seconds` when the provider gave a wait)
          +, `llm_invalid_output` and `llm_output_truncated` and `llm_err
          +or`                                                           
      530 +  502, `llm_unavailable` and `llm_busy` 503, `llm_timeout` 504
          +, `internal` 500 (details: the request id, never the cause).  
      531 +  Errors the API does not know are logged once with the reques
          +t id and answered as `internal`; provider error text and SQL n
          +ever                                                          
      532 +  reach the client. A request that fails to generate leaves it
          +s draft behind and returns `details.campaign_id`, so it can be
      533 +  retried with `POST /api/campaigns/{id}/segment`. Limits: bod
          +y 64 KiB, brief 2,000 characters (not bytes), list limit 1-200
          +.                                                             
      534 +- **Why:** the UI maps codes to its own wording and must not p
          +arse sentences. A draft that survives a failed generation is  
      535 +  cheaper than losing the brief the marketer typed.           
      536 +                                                              
      537 +### The content stream: reconnect-safe, cancel-safe, never blo
          +cking the model                                               
      538 +- **Decision:** `GET /api/campaigns/{id}/content/stream` is Se
          +rver-Sent Events: `attempt`, then `delta {field, text}` (or `r
          +aw`),                                                         
      539 +  then `done {campaign}` or `error {error}`, always exactly on
          +e of the last two. The first line is `retry: 3600000`.        
      540 +  - A campaign that is already `content_ready` is only generat
          +ed again with `?regenerate=true`; otherwise 409 `content_exist
          +s`                                                            
      541 +    before the model is called. A browser's `EventSource` reco
          +nnects by itself when the connection drops and again after the
      542 +    server ends a stream; without the rule that would spend cr
          +edit and overwrite hand edits.                                
      543 +  - The save is conditional on the version the stream started 
          +at: an edit made while the model writes wins, the stream ends 
          +with                                                          
      544 +    `state_changed`, and the generated text is dropped. The sa
          +ve does not depend on the connection: a generation that finish
          +ed                                                            
      545 +    is saved even if the client left in that instant (it was p
          +aid for).                                                     
      546 +  - The model's read loop calls an `onEvent` that only puts th
          +e event in a buffer (256). A client that does not read fills i
          +t, and                                                        
      547 +    the generation is canceled with the cause `client_too_slow
          +` instead of the read loop waiting on the socket.             
      548 +  - Closing the connection cancels the request context, which 
          +cancels the model request; the handler waits for the generatio
          +n                                                             
      549 +    to end before it gives back the per-campaign lock and the 
          +model slot. SIGTERM calls `BeginShutdown` first: open streams 
      550 +    end with `server_shutting_down`, then `http.Server.Shutdow
          +n` waits (15 s) for them.                                     
      551 +  - One generation per campaign at a time (409 `generation_in_
          +progress`, also against `POST .../segment`), kept in memory.  
      552 +- **Why:** each rule closes a concrete failure: a reconnect st
          +orm, a lost edit, a stalled provider read, a leaked slot. The 
      553 +  per-campaign lock is per process: the api is one instance (a
          + second would need an advisory lock like the worker's).       
      554 +- **Alternative:** WebSocket (no automatic reconnect to guard 
          +against, but more machinery), or holding the stream's events u
          +ntil                                                          
      555 +  validation (gives up the live view the assignment asks for).
      556 +                                                              
      557 +### Generation limits close the "no deadline in compose" issue
      558 +- **Decision:** at most `LLM_MAX_CONCURRENCY` (2) generations 
          +run at once; a request waits 5 s for a slot and then gets 503 
      559 +  `llm_busy` with `Retry-After` (and `POST /api/campaigns` tak
          +es the slot before it creates the draft, so a busy server leav
          +es                                                            
      560 +  none behind). A whole segment generation, repairs included, 
          +is limited to 2 minutes and a content generation to 5.        
      561 +- **Why:** with a real key every request spends credit; the re
          +pair loop can make up to nine HTTP requests per task.         
      562 +                                                              
      563 +### Stats: one indexed query, and the worker's state next to t
          +he counts                                                     
      564 +- **Decision:** `StatsSQL` groups `deliveries` by channel, sta
          +tus and `attempts > 0` for one campaign; "retrying" is a queue
          +d                                                             
      565 +  delivery that has been tried. The index is `(campaign_id, st
          +atus, channel, attempts)` (migration 0005) and the old        
      566 +  `(campaign_id, status)` index is dropped, being its prefix. 
          +The completion check (`campaign_id = c.id AND status IN       
      567 +  ('queued','sending')`) uses the prefix. `provider.state` per
          + channel comes from `worker_channel_status`: `ok` (fresh repor
          +t,                                                            
      568 +  circuit closed), `provider_unreachable` (fresh report, circu
          +it open or half-open, with the last error) and `worker_down` (
          +no                                                            
      569 +  report, or older than `WORKER_STALE_AFTER`, 10 s, which wins
          + over what an old report says); the age is measured by the    
      570 +  database.                                                   
      571 +- **EXPLAIN, checked in a test with 20 campaigns of 1,000 deli
          +veries:** the stats query is a `Bitmap Index Scan` on the new 
          +index                                                         
      572 +  and never a sequential scan; with bitmap and sequential scan
          +s switched off it is an `Index Only Scan` on the same index,  
      573 +  which shows the index covers the query. The default plan is 
          +a bitmap scan only because the visibility map of this PostgreS
          +QL                                                            
      574 +  17.6 container stays empty even after `VACUUM` (`relallvisib
          +le` 0, `pg_visibility` agrees); autovacuum fills it in a runni
          +ng                                                            
      575 +  database and the planner then prefers the index-only scan. T
          +he completion check is a `Nested Loop Anti Join` with an index
          + scan                                                         
      576 +  on the new index and an index-only subplan for the failed ch
          +eck.                                                          
      577 +- **Why:** the dashboard polls once a second; both queries sta
          +y off the table.                                              
      578 +                                                              
      579 +### Metrics: the api counts requests and generations, the data
          +base counts deliveries, the worker exposes its own            
      580 +- **Decision:** `/metrics` of the api has `scc_http_request_du
          +ration_seconds{method,route,status}` (route pattern, never the
          + raw                                                          
      581 +  path), `scc_llm_calls_total{task,outcome}` with `scc_llm_cal
          +l_duration_seconds` (one per generation, repairs included, out
          +come                                                          
      582 +  from the error code: ok, refused, rate_limited, timeout, inv
          +alid_output, ...) and `scc_deliveries{channel,status}`, which 
          +is                                                            
      583 +  counted by a query when the scrape arrives, so it covers eve
          +ry process that writes deliveries; if that query fails the scr
          +ape                                                           
      584 +  is a 500, not a zero. The worker serves its own `/metrics` a
          +nd `/healthz` on `WORKER_METRICS_ADDR` (`:9091`, inside the co
          +mpose                                                         
      585 +  network only): `scc_worker_attempts_total{channel,result}` (
          +including `refunded`), `scc_worker_circuit_state`,            
      586 +  `scc_worker_channel_lock_held` and `scc_worker_heartbeat_err
          +ors_total`. The worker package defines an `Observer` interface
          + and                                                          
      587 +  does not import Prometheus; `internal/metrics` implements it
          +.                                                             
      588 +- **Why:** deliveries are written by the worker and read by th
          +e api, so neither process could count them alone without the o
          +ther                                                          
      589 +  seeing a partial picture. Using the route pattern keeps the 
          +number of series fixed.                                       
      590 +                                                              
      591 +### Dependencies, pinned                                      
      592 +- `github.com/go-chi/chi/v5 v5.5.0` (released 14 October 2026)
          +: a small router with route patterns for the metrics labels an
          +d                                                             
      593 +  the `{id}` parameter, no dependencies of its own. `github.co
          +m/prometheus/client_golang v1.25.0` (8 October 2026): the     
      594 +  standard client; it brings `client_model`, `common`, `procfs
          +` and `protobuf`, and raises `golang.org/x/sync`, `x/text` and
      595 +  `x/sys` by a minor version. Both are older than a month, sta
          +ble releases, and exact versions in `go.mod`/`go.sum`.        
      596 +- **Alternative:** `net/http` only for routing (no route patte
          +rn for labels without writing it) and a hand-written text form
          +at                                                            
      597 +  for the metrics (more code to get wrong).                   
      598 +                                                              
      599 +### Smaller choices made while building the API               
      600 +- The preview shows the first users of the segment who consent
          +ed to at least one of the campaign's channels; a channel that 
          +is off,                                                       
      601 +  or that the user did not consent to, is `null` in the sample
          +. `GET /api/campaigns` returns summaries (newest first, 50 by 
      602 +  default). A malformed or zero id is a 404. A request cancele
          +d by the client is logged as 499 `client_closed` and never rea
          +d.                                                            
      603 +- `docker-compose.yml` publishes the api on `127.0.0.1:8080` o
          +nly (there is no authentication and a key can spend credit); t
          +he                                                            
      604 +  worker's metrics port is not published. Both have healthchec
          +ks (`/readyz`, `/healthz`).                                   
      605 +- The api reads `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` from the compose environme
          +nt (empty by default) and logs the mode, model and reason at  
      606 +  start-up, never the key.                                    
      607 +

      673  ### LLM layer (internal/llm, internal/compose)
      674 -- **`compose.Content` emits no terminal event and calls `onEve
          -nt` inside the SDK read loop.** After a final error the text  
      675 -  already streamed stays on screen, and a blocking SSE writer 
          -stalls the provider read. The API layer must send a           
      676 -  failure/discard event and keep `onEvent` non-blocking (buffe
          -r, cancel on overflow).                                       
      677 -- **No task-level deadline in compose**: up to 3 repair attemp
          -ts times 3 HTTP tries is 9 requests, about 12 minutes in the  
      678 -  worst case for content. Add a per-task timeout when the API 
          -calls it.                                                     
      679 -- Duplicate JSON keys resolve to last-wins in `segment.Decode`
          - and `content.Parse` (the preview extractor rejects them),    
      680 -  and the stored snapshot is the raw model text, not a re-enco
          -ding of the validated value.                                  
      674 +- Duplicate JSON keys resolve to last-wins in `segment.Decode`
          + and `content.Parse` (the preview extractor rejects them). The
      675 +  stored snapshot is the validated model text; `jsonb` applies
          + the same last-wins rule, so the two agree (see "Approval").  
      676  - The demo mock drops or inverts some criteria: an amount with
           out a direction ("500 TL altında") produces no condition and
      677    no `unsupported_criteria` entry, "son 400 gün" becomes 30 da
           ys, "sepete ürün eklemeyen" selects people who did add to
      678    cart. The mocks also return a bare `ctx.Err()` where the rea
           l client returns `ErrTimeout`. Demo-only, low.

      664    reason is `ErrUnavailable`, and the SDK error stays in the c
           hain of API errors; generated text containing U+FFFD is
      665    rejected; `ANTHROPIC_FALLBACK` is parsed explicitly (a typo 
           turns the fallback off instead of leaving it on).
      666  
      667 +## Fixed after the second review (the API stage)              
      668 +The `silent-failure-hunter` review of the day's code found no 
          +data corruption and no double send; what it found was in error
      669 +reporting and in the stream's write path.                     
      670 +- **Known failures were never logged.** `problemOf` logged onl
          +y unknown errors, so a wrong key or model name (`llm_error`, 5
          +02),                                                          
      671 +  an unavailable provider or a rate limit left only a status l
          +ine. It now logs every 5xx and every `llm_*` answer with the r
          +equest                                                        
      672 +  id and the cause; the client's answer stays generic. Expecte
          +d client mistakes (404, 409, 422) are not logged.             
      673 +- **A database deadline was reported as a slow model** (`error
          +s.Is(err, context.DeadlineExceeded)` in the LLM mapping), and 
          +the                                                           
      674 +  segment was counted and saved under the model's own budget, 
          +so a late answer could be lost. The generation now turns only 
          +its                                                           
      675 +  own deadline into `errTaskTimeout`, a bare deadline is no lo
          +nger an LLM timeout, and counting and saving get a context of 
          +their                                                         
      676 +  own (15 s).                                                 
      677 +- **A reader that stopped reading held the generation lock and
          + a model slot.** Every write to the stream now has a deadline 
      678 +  (`SSEWriteTimeout`, 15 s); a missed deadline is "the client 
          +is gone". A test opens a raw connection, never reads, and requ
          +ires                                                          
      679 +  the model request to be canceled and the campaign to be gene
          +ratable again.                                                
      680 +- **`cause()` replaced any error** by the shutdown, slow-clien
          +t or timeout cause while the context was ending. It now replac
          +es                                                            
      681 +  only context errors, so a real failure (a database error, `s
          +tate_changed`) keeps its own face.                            
      682 +- **Lost outcomes on a gone client:** what a generation produc
          +ed after its client left (a failed save) is logged; a `done` e
          +vent                                                          
      683 +  that cannot be encoded is replaced by an `error` event, so a
          + stream always ends with one of the two; the panic log has a s
          +tack.                                                         
      684 +- **An approval could report failure after it had been applied
          +** (a failed COMMIT reply, or a read-back that failed). Both n
          +ow                                                            
      685 +  return `ErrApprovalUnconfirmed`, answered as 500 `approval_u
          +nconfirmed` ("may have been applied, check before approving   
      686 +  again"); the read-back has a context of its own. The approva
          +l `UPDATE` checks `RowsAffected`, and the transaction is rolle
          +d                                                             
      687 +  back unconditionally (a no-op after COMMIT), so a panic cann
          +ot leave the row lock held (also in `Enqueue`).               
      688 +- **`last_error` carried the request URL.** A transport error 
          +printed `Post "https://user@host/path?token=..."`, which went 
          +into                                                          
      689 +  the table and out through `/stats`. Only the cause is kept (
          +`connection refused`, `timeout`, ...); a test requires a token
          + and                                                          
      690 +  a user name in the URL to be absent.                        
      691 +- `writeJSON` encodes before it sends (an unencodable value is
          + a 500, not a 200 with an empty body); the segment regeneratio
          +n and                                                         
      692 +  the content stream take the per-campaign lock before they re
          +ad the campaign (a stream that finished a moment ago is seen, 
          +so                                                            
      693 +  a reconnecting client cannot spend credit in the gap); a pan
          +ic after the response has started aborts the connection instea
          +d of                                                          
      694 +  writing a JSON error into an event stream.                  
      695 +

           ntry per issue. The first block comes from the

      719 +### Campaign API and worker (second review, design-level point
          +s)                                                            
      720 +- **An approval that queues nothing reports success.** A rule 
          +that matches nobody, or nobody who consented to the chosen    
      721 +  channels, goes `approved` and straight to `completed` with `
          +enqueued` of 0 and no warning, because the warnings cover only
      722 +  "matches everyone" and unsupported criteria. An `empty_audie
          +nce` warning that needs an acknowledgement would fit; it is a 
      723 +  product decision (is a zero-recipient campaign an error or a
          + no-op?). The acknowledgement is also a plain boolean, and the
      724 +  audience is counted again at approval, so the warnings can d
          +iffer from the ones the marketer saw; the version check does n
          +ot                                                            
      725 +  cover that.                                                 
      726 +- **`LLM_MODE=auto` without a key serves the demo mock** (by d
          +esign, logged and shown in `/api/config`). A key that is blank
          + or                                                           
      727 +  misspelled in the compose environment looks like a working s
          +ystem until the badge is read; `LLM_MODE=anthropic` is the str
          +ict                                                           
      728 +  setting.                                                    
      729 +- **While a circuit is open the dispatcher does not touch its 
          +lock connection**, so a dead lock session is noticed at the fi
          +rst                                                           
      730 +  probe, up to the cooldown (10 s) later. If another worker to
          +ok the channel meanwhile, both write `worker_channel_status` f
          +or a                                                          
      731 +  moment and the dashboard can flap; no message can be sent tw
          +ice (the stale holder claims nothing). A `SELECT 1` on the loc
          +k                                                             
      732 +  connection each poll would close the window.                
      733 +- **A result lost on the last attempt can end as `failed` alth
          +ough the provider accepted the message**: if the outcome of th
          +e                                                             
      734 +  confirmation request cannot be written four times, the lease
          + expires and recovery marks it "outcome unknown". The text hed
          +ges                                                           
      735 +  ("may have delivered"), but "provider accepted = database se
          +nt" holds less strictly there; the same wording also covers a 
      736 +  confirmation that was claimed and never sent (a worker kille
          +d in between), where "ended without an answer" is not verified
          +.                                                             
      737 +- **Maintenance failures are log-only** (lease recovery, campa
          +ign completion): no counter or metric like `RecordErrors` and 
      738 +  `HeartbeatErrors`. If they fail for good, rows stay `sending
          +` and campaigns never complete while the heartbeat still says 
          +the                                                           
      739 +  worker is fine.                                             
      740 +- **A segment generation is not tied to the shutdown signal**:
          + one that is running (up to 2 minutes) outlives the 15 s grace
          +, and                                                         
      741 +  its client gets a cut connection instead of `server_shutting
          +_down`; the draft stays, as documented. Only the content strea
          +m                                                             
      742 +  listens to the shutdown.                                    
      743 +

  Ne bitti

    sondaki nokta düzeltildi, tarayıcı scc-dev-password için bir yanlış alarm
    verdi ve o satırı maskeledim.
  - Aşama 1 (altı açık konu):
    - Doğrulama isteği: outcome unknown metniyle failed olur, rate limiter'dan
      geçer.
    - Idempotency key artık DB'nin ürettiği UUID.
    - Devre kesici: devre açıkken dönen hatalar deneme hakkı yakmaz. 20 sn'lik
      kesinti testinde hiçbir teslimat failed olmadı; kesici kapatılınca aynı
      test kırılıyor.
    - Heartbeat tablosu worker_channel_status; /stats bunu provider.state olarak
      döner.
    - channels alanı ve matches_everyone / unsupported_criteria uyarısı.
    - LLM_MODE=auto|anthropic|mock.

      sırasında da korundu.
  - Aşama 2: API, 11 uç ve /api/config ile healthz/readyz/metrics. SSE akışı,
    version kontrollü onay ve snapshot, worker'ın kendi /metrics'i, compose'ta
    api ve uçtan uca test.
  - Aşama 3: silent-failure-hunter bulgularından 10 gerçek hata düzeltildi, 6
    tartışmalı nokta Open issues'a yazıldı. Çözülen eski açık konular karar
    olarak taşındı.

  Üç düzeltmeniz

  - Devre durumu ve heartbeat: /stats kanal başına ok, provider_unreachable ya
    da worker_down dönüyor. Worker'ı durdurunca 16 sn sonra worker_down,
    başlatınca tekrar ok oldu.
  - Index sırası (campaign_id, status, channel, attempts): eski index
    kaldırıldı. EXPLAIN testi bu sorgu için Bitmap Index Scan gösterdi,
    tamamlanma kontrolü Nested Loop Anti Join ile yeni index'i kullandı.
  - content_exists kuralı: tekrar bağlanan istek 409 content_exists aldı, model
    çağrılmadı ve el düzenlemesi korundu. Testte kanıtlı.

  Kısıtlar ve sonuçlar

  - Index-only scan: bu container'da VACUUM sonrası görünürlük haritası boş
    kalıyor, bu yüzden varsayılan plan bitmap. Bitmap ve seq scan kapatılınca
    Index Only Scan çıkıyor; yani index sorguyu kapsıyor. Canlı veritabanında
    autovacuum bunu doldurur.
  - make soak: yeni backoff (15 sn → 2 dk) yüzünden 3,5 dk sürüyor; sonuç PASS,
    1 teslimat beş yanıtsız denemeyle failed oldu.
  - Guard engelleri (dolanmadım):
    - /tmp altında rm engellendi. Yedek kopya orada kaldı: /tmp/outage_test.bak,
      sır içermeyen bir test dosyası; silmenizi isterim.
    - Dotenv adı geçen bir ls | grep engellendi.
    - Metninde dotenv adı geçen iki betik ve docker compose config ifadesi geçen
      bir komut engellendi. Dokümantasyon düzenlemelerini Edit aracıyla yaptım.

  Kalan

  - Open issues'ta karar bekleyenler:
    - Alıcısı olmayan kampanya uyarısız completed oluyor.
    - Anahtarsız auto modu sessizce mock'a düşüyor.
    - Açık devrede ölü kilit bağlantısı en fazla bir cooldown (10 sn) sonra fark
      ediliyor.
    - Son denemede sonuç yazılamazsa failed kalabilir.
    - Bakım hataları yalnızca loglanıyor.
    - Segment üretimi kapanış sinyaline bağlı değil.
  - Frontend'e başlamadım.

  Verdiğim kararlar

  Hepsi DECISIONS.md'de, nedenleri ve alternatifleriyle. Başlıcaları:
  - Segment yeniden üretilince içerik korunur.
